Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on general structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 474. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 931 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to general theory, we decompose the problem into 474 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 931. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
